LOCAL HANDOFF / LIMITATIONS
# ExamDNA BD MCQ repair handoff

Prepared 8 November 2026. Based on the user's interrupted `work.zip` and original repository commit `f59b998fa84a12a7fdcc7a859549951addfe5445`. This package includes repairs already present in the upload plus completed integration fixes and new verification. No production mutations, remote commits, deployment, credential rotation, or paid model generation were performed.

## Validation performed

- `npm run lint`: passed (TypeScript 5.9.3).
- `npm test`: 43 passed, zero failed/skipped. This includes prior helper tests as well as new HTTP and React-component/hook tests; not every test is end-to-end.
- `npm run test:firebase`: 2 passed using Java 21, a loopback Firestore emulator and the demo project ID. Rules deny self-promotion, direct score writes, roster takeover and cross-user reads. Real Firestore transactions were exercised with a 100-question attempt, 100 mistakes, concurrent identical submissions and a reopened adapter.
- `npm run build`: passed. Main JS output remains about 693 kB minified / 172 kB gzip; Vite warns about the chunk exceeding 500 kB. No measured mobile-performance claim.
- Local HTTP server started successfully. Chromium could not launch because the execution environment denied its process singleton socket. No browser screenshot, real OAuth or visual/mobile pass is claimed.

See `validation/` for final test/build output. Emulator identities in tests are fabricated and passed through a test verifier; real Firebase token verification is wired in server.ts but real-user OAuth was not exercised.

## Repair map

| Audit item | Implementation and evidence |
|---|---|
| S01 fixed admin password | Legacy custom-password routes return 410; server verifies Firebase ID tokens with revocation checking. Admin is authorized by trusted custom claim or server-owned admin record, never a submitted email/role. |
| S02 unguarded admin mutations | Every admin handler checks authorization inside its transaction. Imports require complete validated JSON and enter `needs_review`; no default answer A or automatic approval. |
| S03 caller-controlled identity | Backend derives identity from token, overrides stored ID with authenticated UID, validates teacher approval/batch ownership and student membership. Auth response races are rejected in the client request wrapper. |
| S04 permissive enrollment rules | Client writes are denied, including roster, role, question and result writes. Server enrollment adds only authenticated caller. Rules are supplied and emulator-tested, not deployed. |
| S05 predictable correct option | Each attempt persists shuffled options and remapped answer index. Active-exam responses omit answer keys/explanations; grading/review use the same snapshot. |
| S06 stale timer closure | Hook reads latest answer/callback refs, uses an absolute deadline, and synchronously locks submissions. React test changes answers before expiry and verifies exactly one latest-answer submission. |
| S07 failed submit loses work | Browser progress survives failure; errors are visible; retry unlocks; progress is removed only after successful acknowledgement. Notebook no longer reports simulated success after a failed save. |
| S08 shared demo login fallback | Firebase failure surfaces error, without shared credentials. First Google profile creation handles the backend's unsaved profile placeholder, preserving requested teacher signup. Remember-me controls Firebase persistence. |
| S09 volatile real data | Production requires Firestore storage; transactional backend saves immutable attempt/result/notebook/profile changes. Local filesystem adapter exists only for development/test. Guest records use bounded-session, expiring process memory, not production writes. |
| S10 unknown/duplicate attempts | Unknown and wrong-owner attempts rejected; finalization is atomic/idempotent; malformed answer values rejected. Assignment/challenge reopening returns the same attempt/result rather than resetting time and option order. |
| S11 fabricated history | Dashboard consumes authenticated saved results and exact review snapshots. Empty history is explicit. Signed-in demo completions do not increment personal history/streak. |
| S12 inconsistent accuracy | Both hydration and refresh derive weighted score from saved result totals. Navbar uses active-display streak calculation. |
| S13 notebook persistence | One backend owns retry scheduling and attempt counts. Durable restart tests verify retained notebook state. Browser mistake annotations are scoped by user. |

Additional fixes: quick-start forwards board/year filters; all six requested counts are selectable; insufficient pools and network errors are displayed; zero-question partial start is disabled; stale availability responses are discarded; teacher explicitly selects assignment batch instead of silently using the first batch; teacher failures are visible; historical import parses supplied JSON rather than a fixed sample; historical groups derive from reviewed structured metadata; photo UI updates only after save; logout failure is not presented as successful logout.

## Dependencies and startup

The uploaded project had no npm lockfile and an esbuild peer conflict with Vite. The unnecessary direct esbuild pin was removed; React and react-test-renderer are aligned at 19.2.4; TypeScript is pinned to 5.9.3. `package-lock.json` now records the resolved dependency graph. npm is the intended package manager for this bundle; old alternate lockfiles and unused Windows test loader are omitted from the delivery.

Use Node 24 LTS (engines permit Node >=22.12), `npm ci`, then `npm run dev`. `node --import tsx` avoids the tsx CLI's IPC socket requirement. Production `npm start` expects a prior build and host environment `NODE_ENV=production`, `DATA_STORE=firestore`. Production listens on 0.0.0.0 by default; development uses loopback.

`npm run preview` only previews static build output and does not supply the API: use `npm run dev` or the configured production server for functional checks. Runtime dependencies including Vite are currently in dependencies to support the existing development hosting workflow.

## Production integration required before release

1. Back up the existing database and inventory existing user IDs, role fields, approval flags, admin records, batches, assignments, results and mistakes. No automatic migration is included. Legacy custom user IDs and old fixtures may need an explicit reviewed mapping to Firebase UIDs.
2. Configure the server's authorized Application Default Credentials/service identity and the exact Firebase project/database matching `firebase-applet-config.json`. Never ship service-account keys to the browser or commit them. Local email/Google login also requires an authorized server credential setup to verify real tokens.
3. Review/admin-bootstrap trusted admin authorization and teacher approval records. Legacy user document approval flags are retained; existing records must be reviewed before enabling this build. Revoke old sessions/rotate previously exposed credentials as a separately authorized operation.
4. Stage backend and supplied rules together. The strict rules alone would break the old frontend's direct writes. Existing old frontend + newly replaced backend is not a supported mixed deployment.
5. Verify the actual Google authorized domains/providers, login/logout, account switching, teacher/student/admin flows, multi-device behavior, assignment links, offline/reconnect, clock skew and mobile layouts in staging. Only then authorize release.

## Explicit limitations / remaining work

- Browser/live parity is unverified; the original full hands-on audit is not complete. React renderer checks are not a visual browser substitute.
- The server accepts final submissions until 120 seconds after deadline for transport grace. Later submissions get 409 and browser answers are retained. Server-side answer autosave and recovery after a prolonged disconnection past that grace are not implemented. Client countdown uses its own clock against the server deadline, so large device clock skew still needs handling.
- Guest sessions are process-local, expire after 24 hours, and may be evicted after 500 sessions; restart or another server instance loses guest progress. This is intentional temporary demo behavior, not durable authenticated storage. In-session attempt volume is not rate-limited; capacity/rate limits need production review.
- Authenticated Firestore transactions are tested. This does not certify load behavior. Current adapter lists whole collections for several lookups/analytics and history is unpaginated. Add indexed owner/published-exam queries, pagination, retention and operational rate limits before large-scale rollout.
- Existing question-bank editorial accuracy and historical source authenticity were not independently verified. Structured board/year filters correctly yield zero when those fields are absent. No provenance was fabricated and no extra questions were invented to reach counts. Single-subject pools cannot always supply 100 unique questions.
- The prior interrupted rewrite had disabled paid AI generation/coaching. That behavior is retained: the coach route explicitly returns unavailable; bank-only selection does not call a model. Restoring the old AI coach is outside this MCQ repair delivery.
- Imports now deliberately accept complete JSON with numeric answer indices 0–3 and review required. Free-text import is disabled because it had unsafe implied answers. Admin moderation is still necessary.
- Built-in historical “verified” statuses mean application review state, not an independent source certification. Complete past papers and richer analytics are not asserted.
- No production rules deployment, credential setup, backup/migration, Google login, remote push or live deployment occurred.

===== BEGIN FILE 1/32: src/App.tsx =====
import { apiFetch as fetch } from './utils/api.ts';
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { UserRole, StudentProfile, TeacherBatch, UserMistakeEntry, ChallengeData, UserAccount } from './types.ts';
import type { GradedExamResult } from './utils/examScoring.ts';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar.tsx';
import { LandingPage } from './components/LandingPage.tsx';
import { StudentOnboarding } from './components/StudentOnboarding.tsx';
import { StudentDashboard } from './components/StudentDashboard.tsx';
import { ExamScreen, type ExamQuestionView } from './components/ExamScreen.tsx';
import { ExamResultView } from './components/ExamResultView.tsx';
import { MistakeNotebook } from './components/MistakeNotebook.tsx';
import { TeacherDashboard } from './components/TeacherDashboard.tsx';
import { FriendsChallenge } from './components/FriendsChallenge.tsx';
import { HistoricalAnalysisView } from './components/HistoricalAnalysisView.tsx';
import { AiStudyCoachModal } from './components/AiStudyCoachModal.tsx';
import { AdaptivePracticeModal } from './components/AdaptivePracticeModal.tsx';
import { DailyActivitiesModal } from './components/DailyActivitiesModal.tsx';
import { AuthModal } from './components/AuthModal.tsx';
import { AdminDashboard } from './components/AdminDashboard.tsx';
import { RoleSelectModal } from './components/RoleSelectModal.tsx';
import { ExamSetupPage } from './components/ExamSetupPage.tsx';
import { translations, type Language } from './utils/i18n.ts';
import {
  auth,
  onAuthStateChanged,
  getFirestoreUserProfile,
  saveFirestoreUserProfile,
  getFirestoreExamResults,
  getFirestoreMistakes,
  getFirestoreBatches,
} from './config/firebase.ts';
import { signOut as firebaseSignOut } from 'firebase/auth';
import { calculateUpdatedStreak, getActiveDisplayStreak } from './utils/streakCalculator.ts';
import { Sparkles, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';

export default function App() {
  // Language State with persistence (Default: Bangla)
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<Language>(() => {
    return (localStorage.getItem('examdna_lang') as Language) || 'bn';
  });

  const handleLanguageChange = (newLang: Language) => {
    setLang(newLang);
    localStorage.setItem('examdna_lang', newLang);
  };

  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<UserRole>('student');
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<UserAccount | null>(null);
  
  // Modals
  const [isAuthModalOpen, setIsAuthModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [authDefaultRole, setAuthDefaultRole] = useState<'student' | 'teacher'>('student');
  const [isRoleSelectOpen, setIsRoleSelectOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isDailyActivitiesOpen, setIsDailyActivitiesOpen] = useState(false);
  
  // Active View: 'landing' | 'student-dashboard' | 'teacher-dashboard' | 'admin' | 'exam' | 'result' | 'mistakes' | 'challenge' | 'historical' | 'exam-setup'
  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState<string>(() => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 'landing';
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    if (window.location.pathname === '/admin' || params.get('admin') === '1') return 'admin';
    if (params.get('teacher') === '1') return 'teacher-dashboard';
    if (params.get('winner') === '1' || params.get('challenge') === '1') return 'challenge';
    if (params.get('student') === '1' || params.get('autostart') === '1') return 'student-dashboard';
    return 'landing';
  });
  const [showOnboarding, setShowOnboarding] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Student Profile (Defaults to clean guest state)
  const [studentProfile, setStudentProfile] = useState<StudentProfile>({
    id: 'guest-student',
    name: '',
    role: 'student',
    track: 'science',
    examLevel: 'SSC',
    examYear: '2027',
    activeSubject: 'সাধারণ গণিত',
    streakDays: 0,
  });

  // Mistakes state
  const [mistakes, setMistakes] = useState<UserMistakeEntry[]>([]);

  // Batches state
  const [batches, setBatches] = useState<TeacherBatch[]>([]);

  // Active exam state
  const [activeExam, setActiveExam] = useState<{
    id: string;
    title: string;
    questions: ExamQuestionView[];
    timeLimitMinutes: number;
    deadline?: number;
    publishedExamId?: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Active exam result state
  const [activeResult, setActiveResult] = useState<GradedExamResult | null>(null);

  // Challenge state
  const [currentChallenge, setCurrentChallenge] = useState<ChallengeData | null>(null);

  // Modals
  const [isAiCoachOpen, setIsAiCoachOpen] = useState(false);
  const [aiCoachContext, setAiCoachContext] = useState<{ question?: string; explanation?: string }>({});
  const [isAdaptiveModalOpen, setIsAdaptiveModalOpen] = useState(false);

  // Real Authoritative Exam & Performance Statistics (starts at clean 0, no fake stats)
  const [totalExamsCount, setTotalExamsCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [averageAccuracy, setAverageAccuracy] = useState<number>(0);

  const [examHistory, setExamHistory] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  const identityVersion = useRef(0);
  const applyHistory = (results: any[]) => {
    setExamHistory(results); setTotalExamsCount(results.length);
    const total = results.reduce((n, r) => n + r.totalQuestions, 0);
    setAverageAccuracy(total ? Math.round(results.reduce((n, r) => n + r.score, 0) / total * 100) : 0);
  };
  const applyUser = (profile: UserAccount) => {
    setCurrentUser(profile); setUserRole(profile.role);
    setStudentProfile({ id: profile.id, name: profile.name, role: profile.role, track: profile.track || 'science', examLevel: profile.examLevel || 'SSC', examYear: profile.examYear || '2027', activeSubject: profile.subject || 'সাধারণ গণিত', streakDays: profile.streakDays || 0, lastActivityDate: profile.lastActivityDate, photoUrl: profile.photoUrl });
  };

  // Initial Data Fetch & Dynamic Identity Synchronization
  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    const unsubscribeAuth = onAuthStateChanged(auth, async fbUser => {
      const version = ++identityVersion.current;
      setMistakes([]); setBatches([]); setCurrentChallenge(null); setActiveResult(null); applyHistory([]);
      setCurrentUser(null); setActiveExam(null); setUserRole('student');
      setCurrentView(view => ['exam', 'result', 'mistakes', 'challenge'].includes(view) ? 'landing' : view);
      setStudentProfile({ id: 'guest-student', name: '', role: 'student', track: 'science', examLevel: 'SSC', examYear: '2027', activeSubject: 'সাধারণ গণিত', streakDays: 0 });
      if (!fbUser) return;
      try {
        const [profile, history, savedMistakes, savedBatches] = await Promise.all([getFirestoreUserProfile(fbUser.uid), getFirestoreExamResults(fbUser.uid), getFirestoreMistakes(fbUser.uid), getFirestoreBatches()]);
        if (!mounted || version !== identityVersion.current || auth.currentUser?.uid !== fbUser.uid) return;
        if (profile) applyUser(profile);
        applyHistory(history); setMistakes(savedMistakes); setBatches(savedBatches);
        const examId = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('exam');
        if (examId) await loadAndStartPublishedExam(examId);
      } catch (error: any) { if (mounted && version === identityVersion.current) setNotice(error.message); }
    });
    // Check URL params
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const examParam = params.get('exam');
    const challengeParam = params.get('challenge');
    const autostart = params.get('autostart');
    const adminParam = params.get('admin');
    const teacherParam = params.get('teacher');
    const studentParam = params.get('student');

    if (window.location.pathname === '/admin' || adminParam === '1') {
      setCurrentView('admin');
    } else if (teacherParam === '1') {
      setUserRole('teacher');
      setCurrentView('teacher-dashboard');
    } else if (studentParam === '1' || autostart === '1') {
      setUserRole('student');
      setCurrentView('student-dashboard');
    } else if (params.get('winner') === '1' || challengeParam === '1') {
      setCurrentView('challenge');
    } else if (examParam) {
      setNotice('পরীক্ষায় প্রবেশ করতে লগইন করুন।');
    } else if (challengeParam) {
      loadChallengeByCode(challengeParam);
    } else {
      // The server owns the immutable question snapshot and deadline.
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot');
      if (saved) {
        try {
          const snapshot = JSON.parse(saved);
          auth.authStateReady().then(async () => {
            const version = identityVersion.current;
            const owner = auth.currentUser?.uid || 'guest-student';
            if (snapshot.owner !== owner) return;
            const response = await fetch('/api/attempts/' + encodeURIComponent(snapshot.activeExam.id));
            const data = await response.json();
            if (!mounted || version !== identityVersion.current) return;
            if (data.result) { setActiveResult(data.result); setCurrentView('result'); }
            else { setActiveExam({ ...data, id: data.examId }); setCurrentView('exam'); }
          }).catch((error: any) => setNotice(error.message));
        } catch { localStorage.removeItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot'); }
      }
    }

    return () => {
      mounted = false; identityVersion.current++; unsubscribeAuth();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeExam && currentView === 'exam') localStorage.setItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot', JSON.stringify({activeExam, owner: auth.currentUser?.uid || 'guest-student', savedAt: Date.now()}));
  }, [activeExam, currentView]);

  const loadAndStartPublishedExam = async (examId: string) => {
    const version = identityVersion.current;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/exams/${examId}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        if (version !== identityVersion.current) return;
        if (data.result) { setActiveResult(data.result); setCurrentView('result'); return; }
        const examObj = {
          id: data.id,
          title: data.title,
          questions: data.questions,
          timeLimitMinutes: data.timeLimitMinutes,
          deadline: data.deadline,
          publishedExamId: data.publishedExamId,
        };
        setActiveExam(examObj);
        localStorage.setItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot', JSON.stringify({ activeExam: examObj, owner: auth.currentUser?.uid || 'guest-student', savedAt: Date.now() }));
        setCurrentView('exam');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setNotice(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not load exam. Please retry.');
    }
  };

  const loadChallengeByCode = async (code: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/challenges/${code}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setCurrentChallenge(data);
        setCurrentView('challenge');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setNotice(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not load exam. Please retry.');
    }
  };

  // Helper to record seen question IDs to prevent repeats
  const markQuestionsAsSeen = (questionIds: string[]) => {
    try {
      const seen: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('examdna_seen_questions') || '[]');
      const updated = Array.from(new Set([...seen, ...questionIds]));
      // Keep most recent 150 IDs
      localStorage.setItem('examdna_seen_questions', JSON.stringify(updated.slice(-150)));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  };

  // Start Diagnostic Exam (With non-repeating question protection)
  const handleStartDiagnostic = async (selectedSubject: string = 'all') => {
    try {
      const seenIds: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('examdna_seen_questions') || '[]');
      const res = await fetch('/api/exams/diagnostic', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          subject: selectedSubject,
          count: 12,
          recentQuestionIds: seenIds,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (!Array.isArray(data.questions) || !data.questions.length) throw new Error(data.message || 'No matching questions available.');
      markQuestionsAsSeen(data.questions.map((q: any) => q.id));

      const examObj = {
        id: data.examId,
        title: data.title,
        questions: data.questions,
        timeLimitMinutes: data.timeLimitMinutes,
          deadline: data.deadline,
      };
      setActiveExam(examObj);
      localStorage.setItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot', JSON.stringify({ activeExam: examObj, owner: auth.currentUser?.uid || 'guest-student', savedAt: Date.now() }));
      setCurrentView('exam');
    } catch (err) {
      setNotice(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not start exam. Please retry.');
    }
  };

  // Start 5-question Demo Exam (For Public Visitors)
  const handleTryDemoExam = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/exams/demo');
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        const examObj = {
          id: data.examId,
          title: data.title,
          questions: data.questions,
          timeLimitMinutes: data.timeLimitMinutes,
          deadline: data.deadline,
          isDemo: true,
        };
        setActiveExam(examObj);
        setCurrentView('exam');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setNotice(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not start demo');
    }
  };

  // Start Custom 8-step Exam
  const handleStartCustomExam = async (config: {
    level: string;
    subject: string;
    chapters: string[];
    questionSource: string;
    count: number;
    difficulty: string;
    duration: number;
    allowPartial?: boolean;
  }) => {
    try {
      const seenIds: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('examdna_seen_questions') || '[]');
      const res = await fetch('/api/exams/custom', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...config,
          recentQuestionIds: seenIds,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.error === 'insufficient_questions') {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: data.error,
          availableCount: data.availableCount,
          requestedCount: data.requestedCount,
          message: data.message,
        };
      }

      if (!data.questions || data.questions.length === 0) {
        return { success: false, error: 'no_questions' };
      }

      if (!Array.isArray(data.questions) || !data.questions.length) throw new Error(data.message || 'No matching questions available.');
      markQuestionsAsSeen(data.questions.map((q: any) => q.id));

      const examObj = {
        id: data.examId,
        title: data.title,
        questions: data.questions,
        timeLimitMinutes: data.timeLimitMinutes,
          deadline: data.deadline,
      };
      setActiveExam(examObj);
      localStorage.setItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot', JSON.stringify({ activeExam: examObj, owner: auth.currentUser?.uid || 'guest-student', savedAt: Date.now() }));
      setCurrentView('exam');
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Custom exam error:', err);
      return { success: false, error: 'network_error' };
    }
  };

  // Start Adaptive Practice
  const handleStartAdaptive = async (count: number = 10) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/adaptive/generate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ userId: studentProfile.id, count }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (!Array.isArray(data.questions) || !data.questions.length) throw new Error(data.message || 'No matching questions available.');
      markQuestionsAsSeen(data.questions.map((q: any) => q.id));

      const examObj = {
        id: data.examId,
        title: data.title,
        questions: data.questions,
        timeLimitMinutes: data.timeLimitMinutes,
          deadline: data.deadline,
      };
      setActiveExam(examObj);
      localStorage.setItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot', JSON.stringify({ activeExam: examObj, owner: auth.currentUser?.uid || 'guest-student', savedAt: Date.now() }));
      setCurrentView('exam');
    } catch (err) {
      setNotice(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not start exam. Please retry.');
    }
  };

  // Submit Exam
  const handleSubmitExam = async (answers: Record<string, number>, timeSpentSeconds: number) => {
    const version = identityVersion.current;
    try {
      const questionIds = activeExam?.questions.map((q) => q.id);
      const res = await fetch('/api/exams/submit', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          examId: activeExam?.id,
          publishedExamId: activeExam?.publishedExamId,
          answers,
          timeSpentSeconds,
          userId: studentProfile.id,
          questionIds,
        }),
      });

      const result: GradedExamResult = await res.json();
      if (version !== identityVersion.current) return;
      setActiveResult(result);

      localStorage.removeItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot');
      setCurrentView('result');
      // Saving was acknowledged by the transactional backend before this result appeared.
      try {
        const updatedMistakes = await (await fetch('/api/mistakes')).json();
        if (version !== identityVersion.current) return;
        setMistakes(updatedMistakes);
        if (auth.currentUser) {
          const [history, profile] = await Promise.all([getFirestoreExamResults(auth.currentUser.uid), getFirestoreUserProfile(auth.currentUser.uid)]);
          if (version !== identityVersion.current) return;
          applyHistory(history); if (profile) applyUser(profile);
        }
      } catch (error: any) { setNotice('Result saved. Refresh of progress failed: ' + error.message); }
      if (version === identityVersion.current) setCurrentView('result');
    } catch (err) {
      throw err;
    }
  };

  // Retry Question in Mistake Notebook
  const handleRetryQuestion = async (questionId: string, selectedOpt: number) => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/mistakes/retry', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        userId: studentProfile.id,
        questionId,
        selectedOptionIndex: selectedOpt,
      }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();

    // Refresh mistakes list
    const mistRes = await fetch(`/api/mistakes?userId=${studentProfile.id}`);
    const updatedMistakes = await mistRes.json();
    setMistakes(updatedMistakes);

    return data;
  };

  // Create Batch
  const handleCreateBatch = async (batchData: Partial<TeacherBatch>) => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/batches', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(batchData),
    });
    const newBatch = await res.json();
    setBatches((prev) => [...prev, newBatch]);
    return newBatch;
  };

  // Join Batch
  const handleJoinBatch = async (code: string) => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/batches/join', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        code,
        studentName: studentProfile.name,
        studentId: studentProfile.id,
      }),
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const bRes = await fetch('/api/batches');
      const bData = await bRes.json();
      setBatches(bData);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  // Teacher Generate Exam
  const handleTeacherGenerateExam = async (params: {
    subject: string;
    chapter: string;
    topic: string;
    count: number;
    difficulty: string;
    patternType: string;
  }) => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/teacher/generate-exam', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(params),
    });
    return await res.json();
  };

  // Teacher Publish Exam
  const handleTeacherPublishExam = async (examPayload: any) => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/teacher/publish-exam', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(examPayload),
    });
    const published = await res.json();

    const bRes = await fetch('/api/batches');
    const bData = await bRes.json();
    setBatches(bData);

    return published;
  };

  // Friends Challenge Create
  const handleCreateChallenge = async (data: {
    subject: string;
    chapter: string;
    topic: string;
    creatorName: string;
  }) => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/challenges/create', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        ...data,
        creatorId: studentProfile.id,
      }),
    });
    return await res.json();
  };

  // Friends Challenge Start taking
  const handleStartTakingChallenge = async (challenge: ChallengeData) => {
    try {
      const data = await (await fetch('/api/challenges/' + encodeURIComponent(challenge.code))).json();
      if (data.result) { setActiveResult(data.result); setCurrentView('result'); return; }
      setActiveExam({ ...data, id: data.examId }); setCurrentView('exam');
    } catch (error: any) { setNotice(error.message); }
  };

  // Open AI Coach with context
  const handleOpenAiCoachWithContext = (questionText: string, explanation: string) => {
    setAiCoachContext({ question: questionText, explanation });
    setIsAiCoachOpen(true);
  };

  // Auth Success Callback
  const handleAuthSuccess = async (user: UserAccount, _token: string) => {
    const version = ++identityVersion.current;
    setMistakes([]); applyHistory([]); setActiveResult(null); setActiveExam(null);
    if (!auth.currentUser) { setCurrentUser(null); setCurrentView('student-dashboard'); return; }
    try {
      const [profile, history, savedMistakes, savedBatches] = await Promise.all([getFirestoreUserProfile(user.id), getFirestoreExamResults(user.id), getFirestoreMistakes(user.id), getFirestoreBatches()]);
      if (version !== identityVersion.current || auth.currentUser?.uid !== user.id || !profile) return;
      applyUser(profile); applyHistory(history); setMistakes(savedMistakes); setBatches(savedBatches);
      setCurrentView(profile.role === 'admin' ? 'admin' : profile.role === 'teacher' ? 'teacher-dashboard' : 'student-dashboard');
    } catch (error: any) { setNotice(error.message); }
  };

  // Student Photo Update Handler
  const handleUpdateStudentPhoto = async (newPhotoUrl: string | null) => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    const version = identityVersion.current;
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/user/photo', {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ photoUrl: newPhotoUrl }),
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (version === identityVersion.current) applyUser(data.user);
    } catch (error: any) { setNotice(error.message); }
  };

  // Logout Callback
  const handleLogout = async () => {
    try {
      await firebaseSignOut(auth);
    } catch (err) {
      setNotice('Logout failed. Please retry.');
      return;
    }
    const token = localStorage.getItem('examdna_token');
    if (token) {
      await fetch('/api/auth/logout', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
    identityVersion.current++;
    localStorage.removeItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot');
    setActiveExam(null); applyHistory([]);
    localStorage.removeItem('examdna_token');
    setCurrentUser(null);
    setUserRole('student');
    setStudentProfile({
      id: 'guest-student',
      name: '',
      role: 'student',
      track: 'science',
      examLevel: 'SSC',
      examYear: '2027',
      activeSubject: 'সাধারণ গণিত',
      streakDays: 0,
    });
    setTotalExamsCount(0);
    setAverageAccuracy(0);
    setMistakes([]);
    setActiveResult(null);
    setCurrentView('landing');
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8FAFC]">
      {notice && <div role="alert" className="p-4 bg-amber-50 text-amber-900">{notice}<button className="ml-4 underline" onClick={() => setNotice('')}>Dismiss</button></div>}
      {currentView === 'teacher-dashboard' && !currentUser && <div className="p-6"><p>শিক্ষক হিসেবে লগইন করুন।</p><button onClick={() => { setAuthDefaultRole('teacher'); setIsAuthModalOpen(true); }}>লগইন</button></div>}
      {/* Top Navigation Bar */}
      {currentView !== 'admin' && (
        <Navbar
          currentView={currentView}
          onNavigate={(view) => {
            if (view === 'diagnostic-setup') {
              handleStartDiagnostic();
            } else {
              setCurrentView(view);
            }
          }}
          lang={lang}
          onLanguageChange={handleLanguageChange}
          currentUser={currentUser}
          onOpenAuthModal={() => {
            setAuthDefaultRole('student');
            setIsAuthModalOpen(true);
          }}
          onOpenRoleSelect={() => setIsRoleSelectOpen(true)}
          onLogout={handleLogout}
          streakDays={getActiveDisplayStreak(studentProfile.streakDays, studentProfile.lastActivityDate)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Main Views Container */}
      <main className="flex-1">
        {currentView === 'landing' && (
          <LandingPage
            onStartExam={() => setCurrentView('exam-setup')}
            onDemoExamClick={handleTryDemoExam}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            totalExamsCount={totalExamsCount}
            averageAccuracy={averageAccuracy}
            onUpdateStudentPhoto={handleUpdateStudentPhoto}
            onStudentClick={() => {
              if (currentUser?.role === 'student') {
                setCurrentView('student-dashboard');
              } else {
                setAuthDefaultRole('student');
                setIsAuthModalOpen(true);
              }
            }}
            onTeacherClick={() => {
              if (currentUser?.role === 'teacher') {
                setCurrentView('teacher-dashboard');
              } else {
                setAuthDefaultRole('teacher');
                setIsAuthModalOpen(true);
              }
            }}
            onNavigate={setCurrentView}
            lang={lang}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'exam-setup' && (
          <ExamSetupPage
            lang={lang}
            onStartExam={handleStartCustomExam}
            onBack={() => setCurrentView('student-dashboard')}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'admin' && (
          <AdminDashboard
            lang={lang}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            onLogout={handleLogout}
            onAdminAuthSuccess={handleAuthSuccess}
            onExitAdminView={() => setCurrentView('landing')}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'student-dashboard' && (
          <StudentDashboard
            profile={studentProfile}
            examHistory={examHistory}
            onReviewResult={result => { setActiveResult(result); setCurrentView('result'); }}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            mistakes={mistakes}
            totalExamsCount={totalExamsCount}
            averageAccuracy={averageAccuracy}
            onStartDiagnostic={() => handleStartDiagnostic('all')}
            onStartAdaptive={() => setIsAdaptiveModalOpen(true)}
            onOpenMistakes={() => setCurrentView('mistakes')}
            onOpenChallenge={() => setCurrentView('challenge')}
            onOpenAiCoach={() => {
              setAiCoachContext({});
              setIsAiCoachOpen(true);
            }}
            onOpenDailyActivities={() => setIsDailyActivitiesOpen(true)}
            onJoinBatch={handleJoinBatch}
            onNavigateToSetup={() => setCurrentView('exam-setup')}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'exam' && activeExam && (
          <ExamScreen
            key={activeExam.id}
            deadline={activeExam.deadline}
            examId={activeExam.id}
            title={activeExam.title}
            questions={activeExam.questions}
            timeLimitMinutes={activeExam.timeLimitMinutes}
            publishedExamId={activeExam.publishedExamId}
            onSubmitExam={handleSubmitExam}
            onExit={() => {
              localStorage.removeItem('examdna_active_exam_snapshot');
              setCurrentView('student-dashboard');
            }}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'result' && activeResult && (
          <ExamResultView
            result={activeResult}
            onOpenMistakes={() => setCurrentView('mistakes')}
            onStartAdaptive={() => setIsAdaptiveModalOpen(true)}
            onRetake={() => handleStartDiagnostic('all')}
            onBackToDashboard={() => setCurrentView('student-dashboard')}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'mistakes' && (
          <MistakeNotebook
            key={studentProfile.id}
            mistakes={mistakes}
            onRetryQuestion={handleRetryQuestion}
            onOpenAiCoachWithQuestion={handleOpenAiCoachWithContext}
            onBack={() => setCurrentView('student-dashboard')}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'teacher-dashboard' && currentUser && ['teacher', 'admin'].includes(currentUser.role) && (
          <TeacherDashboard
            batches={batches}
            onCreateBatch={handleCreateBatch}
            onGenerateExam={handleTeacherGenerateExam}
            onPublishExam={handleTeacherPublishExam}
            onBackToStudent={() => {
              setUserRole('student');
              setCurrentView('student-dashboard');
            }}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'challenge' && (
          <FriendsChallenge
            currentUser={currentUser}
            studentProfile={studentProfile}
            currentChallenge={currentChallenge}
            onCreateChallenge={handleCreateChallenge}
            onStartTakingChallenge={handleStartTakingChallenge}
            onBack={() => setCurrentView('student-dashboard')}
          />
        )}

        {currentView === 'historical' && (
          <HistoricalAnalysisView
            userRole={userRole}
            onBack={() => setCurrentView('student-dashboard')}
          />
        )}
      </main>

      {/* Role Selection Modal (Screen 2: Get Started) */}
      <RoleSelectModal
        isOpen={isRoleSelectOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsRoleSelectOpen(false)}
        lang={lang}
        onSelectRole={(selectedRole) => {
          setIsRoleSelectOpen(false);
          setAuthDefaultRole(selectedRole);
          setIsAuthModalOpen(true);
        }}
        onOpenLogin={() => {
          setIsRoleSelectOpen(false);
          setAuthDefaultRole('student');
          setIsAuthModalOpen(true);
        }}
      />

      {/* Student & Teacher Auth Modal (Screens 3 & 4) */}
      <AuthModal
        isOpen={isAuthModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(false)}
        lang={lang}
        defaultRole={authDefaultRole}
        onAuthSuccess={handleAuthSuccess}
      />

      {/* Student Onboarding Modal */}
      {showOnboarding && (
        <StudentOnboarding
          onComplete={(newProfile) => {
            setStudentProfile(newProfile);
            setShowOnboarding(false);
            handleStartDiagnostic('all');
          }}
          onCancel={() => setShowOnboarding(false)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Floating AI Study Coach Quick-Launcher */}
      {!isAiCoachOpen && currentView !== 'exam' && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            setAiCoachContext({});
            setIsAiCoachOpen(true);
          }}
          className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-40 px-4 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 via-indigo-600 to-blue-700 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-indigo-700 text-white rounded-full shadow-xl shadow-blue-500/30 border border-blue-400/40 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-all hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 group cursor-pointer"
          title="ExamDNA বাংলা AI স্টাডি কোচ"
        >
          <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-200 animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold tracking-tight">AI স্টাডি কোচ</span>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400/20 text-emerald-200 border border-emerald-300/30">
            $০ ফ্রি
          </span>
        </button>
      )}

      {/* Bangla AI Study Coach Modal (Multi-turn Gemini Chatbot with Zero API Cost) */}
      <AiStudyCoachModal
        isOpen={isAiCoachOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAiCoachOpen(false)}
        mistakes={mistakes}
        initialQuestion={aiCoachContext.question}
        initialExplanation={aiCoachContext.explanation}
      />

      {/* Adaptive Practice Launcher Modal */}
      <AdaptivePracticeModal
        isOpen={isAdaptiveModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAdaptiveModalOpen(false)}
        mistakes={mistakes}
        onConfirmStart={(count) => handleStartAdaptive(count)}
      />

      {/* Daily Micro-Activities Modal (10 Min, Mistake Detective, Rematch) */}
      <DailyActivitiesModal
        isOpen={isDailyActivitiesOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsDailyActivitiesOpen(false)}
        mistakes={mistakes}
        availableQuestions={[]}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


===== END FILE: src/App.tsx =====

===== BEGIN FILE 2/32: src/components/FounderSection.tsx =====
import { apiFetch as fetch } from '../utils/api.ts';
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { ShieldCheck, Sparkles, Heart, Award, ArrowUpRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'motion/react';
import { translations, Language } from '../utils/i18n';

interface FounderSectionProps {
  lang: Language;
  onExploreExams?: () => void;
}

const DEFAULT_FOUNDER_IMAGE = '/assets/founder-mustafizur-rahman.jpeg';

export const FounderSection: React.FC<FounderSectionProps> = ({ lang, onExploreExams }) => {
  const t = translations[lang];
  const [photoUrl, setPhotoUrl] = useState<string>(DEFAULT_FOUNDER_IMAGE);

  // Load saved founder photo from backend or default to founder image
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/founder/photo')
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data.photoUrl) {
          setPhotoUrl(data.photoUrl);
        } else {
          setPhotoUrl(DEFAULT_FOUNDER_IMAGE);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        setPhotoUrl(DEFAULT_FOUNDER_IMAGE);
      });
  }, []);

  return (
    <section id="founder-section" className="py-20 bg-gradient-to-b from-white via-blue-50/40 to-slate-50 border-t border-blue-100/60 relative overflow-hidden">
      {/* Subtle background ambient glows */}
      <div className="absolute top-1/4 -left-32 w-96 h-96 bg-blue-200/25 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-10 right-0 w-80 h-80 bg-indigo-200/20 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 relative z-10">
        {/* Section Header */}
        <div className="text-center max-w-3xl mx-auto mb-16">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-100/80 border border-blue-200 text-blue-700 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider mb-4 shadow-sm">
            <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600 animate-pulse" />
            <span>{t.founderBadge}</span>
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-extrabold text-[#0F172A] tracking-tight mb-4">
            {t.founderTitle}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-base sm:text-lg text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
            {t.founderSubtitle}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Content Card with Two Columns */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-8 sm:p-12 shadow-xl shadow-blue-500/5 border border-blue-100/80 backdrop-blur-sm transition-all hover:shadow-2xl hover:shadow-blue-500/10">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10 items-center">
            
            {/* Founder Photo & Framing Column (5 Cols) */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col items-center">
              <div className="relative group">
                {/* White to soft-blue gradient frame glow */}
                <div className="absolute -inset-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-200 via-indigo-100 to-sky-200 rounded-3xl blur-md opacity-75 group-hover:opacity-100 transition duration-500" />
                
                {/* Inner Card Framing */}
                <div className="relative bg-gradient-to-tr from-white via-blue-50/60 to-white p-3 rounded-2xl border border-blue-200/70 shadow-lg">
                  <div className="w-64 h-80 sm:w-72 sm:h-96 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-b from-blue-50 to-slate-100 border border-blue-100 flex items-center justify-center relative shadow-inner">
                    <img
                      src={photoUrl || DEFAULT_FOUNDER_IMAGE}
                      alt="Mustafizur Rahman - Founder & Vision Architect, ExamDNA BD"
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover object-top transition duration-500 group-hover:scale-105"
                      onError={(e) => {
                        (e.currentTarget as HTMLImageElement).src = '/assets/aistudio/WhatsApp_Image_2026-11-04_at_5.53.28_PM_d85e6cfb.jpeg';
                      }}
                    />

                    {/* Verified Badge */}
                    <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full shadow-md border border-blue-100 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700">
                      <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
                      <span>{lang === 'bn' ? 'যাচাইকৃত প্রতিষ্ঠাতা' : 'Verified Founder'}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Founder Identity Card below Portrait (Replacing upload button) */}
              <div className="mt-5 text-center">
                <h4 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#0F172A] tracking-tight">Mustafizur Rahman</h4>
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-600 mt-0.5">{t.founderRole}</p>
                <div className="mt-2.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-blue-50/80 border border-blue-200/80 rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium text-blue-700">
                  <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-blue-600" />
                  <span>{lang === 'bn' ? 'উদ্যোক্তা ও প্রধান রূপকার' : 'Founder & Vision Architect'}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Founder Message & Vision Column (7 Cols) */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-6">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <h3 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#0F172A]">
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'আমাদের প্রত্যয় ও পেছনের গল্প' : 'Our Mission & Story'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-600">
                  ExamDNA BD &bull; {lang === 'bn' ? 'বাংলাদেশি শিক্ষার্থীদের জন্য তৈরি' : 'Engineered for Bangladeshi Students'}
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Quote Box with Gentle Left Border */}
              <div className="relative pl-6 border-l-4 border-blue-500 py-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-50/50 to-transparent rounded-r-2xl">
                <p className="text-base sm:text-lg text-slate-700 italic leading-relaxed">
                  "{t.founderQuote}"
                </p>
                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-600" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#0F172A]">Mustafizur Rahman</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">&bull; {t.founderRole}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Two Core Pillars */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-2">
                <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-200/80 hover:bg-blue-50/40 hover:border-blue-200 transition-all">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-blue-100 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center mb-2.5">
                    <Award className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] mb-1">{t.founderPillar1Title}</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{t.founderPillar1Desc}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-200/80 hover:bg-blue-50/40 hover:border-blue-200 transition-all">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-blue-100 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center mb-2.5">
                    <Heart className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500" />
                  </div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] mb-1">{t.founderPillar2Title}</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{t.founderPillar2Desc}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Call to action inside founder section */}
              {onExploreExams && (
                <div className="pt-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onExploreExams}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-medium text-sm rounded-xl shadow-md shadow-blue-500/20 transition-all hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-blue-500/30"
                  >
                    <span>{t.heroStartExamBtn}</span>
                    <ArrowUpRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

===== END FILE: src/components/FounderSection.tsx =====

===== BEGIN FILE 3/32: src/components/ExamScreen.tsx =====
import { useExamSession } from '../utils/useExamSession.ts';
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Clock,
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Check,
  Send,
  Calculator,
  Bookmark,
  RotateCcw,
  AlertCircle,
  HelpCircle,
  X
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ScientificCalculatorModal } from './ScientificCalculatorModal';

export interface ExamQuestionView {
  id: string;
  subject?: string;
  chapter?: string;
  topic?: string;
  questionText: string;
  options: [string, string, string, string];
  difficulty?: string;
}

interface ExamScreenProps {
  examId: string;
  title: string;
  questions: ExamQuestionView[];
  timeLimitMinutes: number;
  publishedExamId?: string;
  deadline?: number;
  onSubmitExam: (answers: Record<string, number>, timeSpentSeconds: number) => Promise<void>;
  onExit: () => void;
}

export const ExamScreen: React.FC<ExamScreenProps> = ({
  examId,
  title,
  questions,
  timeLimitMinutes,
  publishedExamId,
  deadline,
  onSubmitExam,
  onExit,
}) => {
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0);
  const [markedForReview, setMarkedForReview] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [showConfirmModal, setShowConfirmModal] = useState(false);
  const [isCalculatorOpen, setIsCalculatorOpen] = useState(false);
  const totalQuestions = questions.length;
  const { answers, setAnswers, timeLeftSeconds, isSubmitting, error: submitError, submit: handleManualSubmit } = useExamSession(examId, timeLimitMinutes, deadline, onSubmitExam);
  const currentQ = questions[currentIndex] || questions[0];
  const answeredCount = Object.keys(answers).length;

  const formatTimer = (totalSec: number) => {
    const hours = Math.floor(totalSec / 3600);
    const mins = Math.floor((totalSec % 3600) / 60);
    const secs = totalSec % 60;
    return `${hours.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${mins.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  const isTimeCritical = timeLeftSeconds < 180;

  const selectOption = (optIndex: number) => {
    if (timeLeftSeconds <= 0 || isSubmitting) return;
    setAnswers((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [currentQ.id]: optIndex,
    }));
  };

  const clearSelection = () => {
    if (timeLeftSeconds <= 0 || isSubmitting) return;
    setAnswers((prev) => {
      const copy = { ...prev };
      delete copy[currentQ.id];
      return copy;
    });
  };

  const toggleReview = () => {
    setMarkedForReview((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [currentQ.id]: !prev[currentQ.id],
    }));
  };

  const isCurrentMarked = !!markedForReview[currentQ?.id];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] flex flex-col justify-between">
      {submitError && <div role="alert" className="p-4 bg-red-50 text-red-800">{submitError} — উত্তরগুলো রাখা আছে। <button disabled={isSubmitting} onClick={handleManualSubmit} className="underline font-bold">আবার জমা দিন</button></div>}
      
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      {/* TOP HEADER: Breadcrumb, Timer Pill, Blue Submit Button         */}
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-blue-100 px-4 sm:px-8 py-3.5 shadow-2xs">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          
          {/* Left Breadcrumb */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onExit}
              className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-800 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
              title="Exit exam"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
            <div>
              <div className="text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] flex items-center gap-2">
                <span>{currentQ?.subject || 'Mathematics'}</span>
                <span className="text-slate-300">•</span>
                <span className="text-blue-600 font-semibold">{currentQ?.chapter || 'Algebra'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
                Answered: <strong className="text-blue-600 font-mono-numbers">{answeredCount}</strong> / {totalQuestions} questions
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right: Live Countdown Badge & Blue Submit Button */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full border text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold font-mono-numbers shadow-2xs ${
                isTimeCritical
                  ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 border-rose-300 animate-pulse'
                  : 'bg-blue-50/80 text-blue-700 border-blue-200'
              }`}
            >
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
              <span>{formatTimer(timeLeftSeconds)}</span>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowConfirmModal(true)}
              className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm shadow-md shadow-blue-500/25 transition-all cursor-pointer"
            >
              Submit
            </button>
          </div>

        </div>
      </header>

      {/* ============================================================== */}
      {/* QUESTION NAVIGATOR PILL STRIP (1 to 20)                        */}
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      <div className="bg-white border-b border-slate-100 px-4 sm:px-8 py-3">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 scrollbar-none">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 mr-2 shrink-0">
            Question {currentIndex + 1} of {totalQuestions}:
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {questions.map((q, idx) => {
              const isAnswered = answers[q.id] !== undefined;
              const isCurrent = idx === currentIndex;
              const isFlagged = markedForReview[q.id];

              return (
                <button
                  key={q.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setCurrentIndex(idx)}
                  className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs font-mono-numbers shrink-0 transition-all ${
                    isCurrent
                      ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-500/25 ring-2 ring-blue-300'
                      : isFlagged
                      ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300'
                      : isAnswered
                      ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-300'
                      : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {idx + 1}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================== */}
      {/* MAIN QUESTION CARD WITH FLOATING TOOLS (Screen 9)             */}
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      <main className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-8 flex-1 w-full flex flex-col justify-center">
        
        <div className="relative bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-10 border border-blue-100 shadow-xl shadow-blue-500/5">
          
          {/* Floating Side Tools matching Screen 9 */}
          <div className="absolute top-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsCalculatorOpen(true)}
              className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-blue-50 border border-slate-200 hover:border-blue-300 text-slate-600 hover:text-blue-600 transition-colors shadow-2xs"
              title="Open Scientific Calculator"
            >
              <Calculator className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={toggleReview}
              className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition-colors shadow-2xs ${
                isCurrentMarked
                  ? 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-600'
                  : 'bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border-slate-200 text-slate-500'
              }`}
              title="Bookmark for review"
            >
              <Bookmark className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={clearSelection}
              className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 transition-colors shadow-2xs"
              title="Clear selection"
            >
              <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Question Title & Number */}
          <div className="mb-8 pt-12 sm:pt-0 sm:pr-32">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-blue-100 inline-block mb-3">
              Question {currentIndex + 1}
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-lg sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[#0F172A] leading-relaxed">
              Q{currentIndex + 1}. {currentQ?.questionText}
            </h2>
          </div>

          {/* 4 Options Grid (A, B, C, D) matching Screen 9 */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            {currentQ?.options.map((optionText, optIdx) => {
              const isSelected = answers[currentQ.id] === optIdx;
              const optionLetter = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'][optIdx];

              return (
                <button
                  key={optIdx}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => selectOption(optIdx)}
                  className={`p-5 rounded-2xl border-2 text-left transition-all flex items-center justify-between group cursor-pointer ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-50/70 shadow-md shadow-blue-500/10'
                      : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-blue-200 hover:bg-slate-50/60'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                    <div
                      className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs font-mono transition-colors ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs'
                          : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 group-hover:bg-blue-100 group-hover:text-blue-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {optionLetter}
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-sm sm:text-base font-semibold text-[#0F172A]">
                      {optionText}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div
                    className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center ${
                      isSelected ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-600' : 'border-slate-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {isSelected && <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white stroke-[3]" />}
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

        </div>

      </main>

      {/* ============================================================== */}
      {/* BOTTOM CONTROL BAR: Previous, Mark for review, Next            */}
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      <footer className="bg-white border-t border-slate-200/80 px-4 sm:px-8 py-4">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={currentIndex === 0}
            onClick={() => setCurrentIndex((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - 1))}
            className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Previous</span>
          </button>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 cursor-pointer select-none">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={isCurrentMarked}
              onChange={toggleReview}
              className="w-4 h-4 rounded text-blue-600 focus:ring-blue-500 border-slate-300"
            />
            <span>Mark for review</span>
          </label>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              if (currentIndex < totalQuestions - 1) {
                setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
              } else {
                setShowConfirmModal(true);
              }
            }}
            className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-blue-500/25 flex items-center gap-2 transition-all cursor-pointer"
          >
            <span>{currentIndex < totalQuestions - 1 ? 'Next' : 'Review & Submit'}</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Scientific Calculator Modal */}
      <ScientificCalculatorModal
        isOpen={isCalculatorOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsCalculatorOpen(false)}
      />

      {/* Confirm Submission Modal */}
      {showConfirmModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 max-w-sm w-full shadow-2xl border border-blue-100 text-center animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Check className="w-6 h-6 stroke-[3]" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-[#0F172A] mb-2">
              Submit Your Exam?
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-6">
              You answered <strong className="text-blue-600 font-mono-numbers">{answeredCount}</strong> out of {totalQuestions} questions. Are you ready to see your final score and mistake breakdown?
            </p>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleManualSubmit}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-colors shadow-md shadow-blue-500/25 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {isSubmitting ? 'Grading Answers...' : 'Yes, Submit Exam'}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowConfirmModal(false)}
                className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 font-semibold text-xs transition-colors"
              >
                Continue Exam
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

===== END FILE: src/components/ExamScreen.tsx =====

===== BEGIN FILE 4/32: src/components/MistakeNotebook.tsx =====
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { UserMistakeEntry } from '../types';
import {
  BookOpen,
  Filter,
  RotateCcw,
  Sparkles,
  CheckCircle2,
  XCircle,
  Clock,
  ArrowRight,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  BrainCircuit,
  HelpCircle,
  ArrowLeft
} from 'lucide-react';

interface MistakeNotebookProps {
  mistakes: UserMistakeEntry[];
  onRetryQuestion: (questionId: string, selectedOpt: number) => Promise<{
    isCorrect: boolean;
    correctOptionIndex: number;
    explanation: string;
    nextReviewAt: string;
    intervalStep: number;
  }>;
  onOpenAiCoachWithQuestion: (questionText: string, explanation: string) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

export const MistakeNotebook: React.FC<MistakeNotebookProps> = ({
  mistakes = [],
  onRetryQuestion,
  onOpenAiCoachWithQuestion,
  onBack,
}) => {
  const [selectedSubject, setSelectedSubject] = useState<string>('all');
  const [selectedChapter, setSelectedChapter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [expandedCardIds, setExpandedCardIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  // Retry modal/card state
  const [activeRetryEntry, setActiveRetryEntry] = useState<UserMistakeEntry | null>(null);
  const [retrySelectedOpt, setRetrySelectedOpt] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [retryResult, setRetryResult] = useState<{ isCorrect: boolean; explanation: string } | null>(null);
  const [retryError, setRetryError] = useState('');
  const [isSubmittingRetry, setIsSubmittingRetry] = useState(false);

  const reasonsKey = `examdna_mistake_reasons_${mistakes[0]?.userId || 'guest'}`;
  // State to track student's self-diagnosed mistake reasons
  const [mistakeReasons, setMistakeReasons] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(reasonsKey) || '{}');
    } catch {
      return {};
    }
  });

  // State to toggle hints
  const [revealedHints, setRevealedHints] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const handleSetReason = (questionId: string, reason: string) => {
    setMistakeReasons((prev) => {
      const updated = { ...prev, [questionId]: reason };
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(reasonsKey, JSON.stringify(updated));
      } catch {}
      return updated;
    });
  };

  const safeMistakes = Array.isArray(mistakes) ? mistakes : [];

  // Honest display: If student has no mistakes, display empty state instead of fake demo items
  const displayMistakes = safeMistakes;

  // Subjects & Chapters for filters
  const allSubjects = Array.from(new Set(displayMistakes.map(m => m.question.subject || 'সাধারণ গণিত')));
  const allChapters = Array.from(new Set(displayMistakes.map(m => m.question.chapter || 'বীজগাণিতিক রাশি')));

  const filteredMistakes = displayMistakes.filter(m => {
    if (selectedSubject !== 'all' && m.question.subject !== selectedSubject) return false;
    if (selectedChapter !== 'all' && m.question.chapter !== selectedChapter) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const toggleExpand = (id: string) => {
    setExpandedCardIds(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [id]: !prev[id],
    }));
  };

  const handleStartRetry = (entry: any) => {
    setActiveRetryEntry(entry);
    setRetrySelectedOpt(null);
    setRetryResult(null); setRetryError('');
  };

  const handleConfirmRetry = async () => {
    if (!activeRetryEntry || retrySelectedOpt === null) return;
    if (isSubmittingRetry) return;
    setRetryError(''); setIsSubmittingRetry(true);
    try {
      const res = await onRetryQuestion(activeRetryEntry.questionId, retrySelectedOpt);
      setRetryResult({
        isCorrect: res.isCorrect,
        explanation: res.explanation,
      });
    } catch (error: any) {
      setRetryError(error.message || 'উত্তর সংরক্ষণ হয়নি। আবার চেষ্টা করুন।');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmittingRetry(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] py-8 sm:py-12 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      {retryError && <p role="alert" className="p-4 bg-red-50 text-red-800">{retryError}</p>}
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        
        {/* Header & Back */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-slate-200">
          <div>
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-rose-600 bg-rose-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-rose-200 mb-2">
              <BookOpen className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Spaced Repetition Notebook</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-[#0F172A] tracking-tight">
              Mistake Notebook
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
              Review every mistake with step-by-step explanations and AI coaching.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onBack}
            className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-xl transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 self-start sm:self-auto"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Back to Dashboard</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Dropdown Filters matching Screen 11 */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-blue-100 shadow-2xs flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            
            {/* Subject Dropdown */}
            <div className="relative">
              <select
                value={selectedSubject}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setSelectedSubject(e.target.value);
                  setSelectedChapter('all');
                }}
                className="appearance-none bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs font-bold text-[#0F172A] rounded-xl pl-3 pr-8 py-2 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <option value="all">All Subjects</option>
                {allSubjects.map((sub) => (
                  <option key={sub} value={sub}>{sub}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute right-2.5 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
            </div>

            {/* Chapter Dropdown */}
            <div className="relative">
              <select
                value={selectedChapter}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedChapter(e.target.value)}
                className="appearance-none bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs font-bold text-[#0F172A] rounded-xl pl-3 pr-8 py-2 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <option value="all">All Chapters</option>
                {allChapters.map((ch) => (
                  <option key={ch} value={ch}>{ch}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute right-2.5 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
            </div>

          </div>

          <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 font-mono-numbers">
            {filteredMistakes.length} mistakes saved
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Mistake Cards matching Screen 11 */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {filteredMistakes.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-10 text-center border border-slate-200/80 space-y-3">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7" />
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">মিস্টেক নোটবুক একদম পরিষ্কার!</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto leading-relaxed">
                তোমার কোনো অমীমাংসিত ভুল রেকর্ড নেই। কোনো পরীক্ষায় ভুল উত্তর দিলে তা এখানে স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে যুক্ত হবে এবং রিভিশনের শিডিউল তৈরি হবে।
              </p>
              <div className="pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onBack}
                  className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  মডেল টেস্ট দিয়ে প্রস্তুতি শুরু করো
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            filteredMistakes.map((m) => {
              const isExpanded = !!expandedCardIds[m.id];
              const q = m.question;
              const currentReason = mistakeReasons[q.id] || '';
              const isHintRevealed = !!revealedHints[q.id];

              const reasonsList = [
                { id: 'concept', label: 'ধারণা বুঝতে পারিনি' },
                { id: 'calculation', label: 'গণনার ভুল' },
                { id: 'misread', label: 'প্রশ্ন ভুল পড়েছি' },
                { id: 'time', label: 'সময় শেষ' },
                { id: 'guessed', label: 'অনুমান করেছি' },
              ];

              return (
                <div
                  key={m.id}
                  className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 border border-blue-100/80 shadow-2xs hover:shadow-md transition-all space-y-4"
                >
                  {/* Header row */}
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                    <div className="space-y-1.5 flex-1">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-blue-100">
                          {q.subject || 'সাধারণ গণিত'} • {q.chapter}
                        </span>
                        {q.topic && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
                            {q.topic}
                          </span>
                        )}
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-purple-700 bg-purple-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold border border-purple-100">
                          রিভিশন ধাপ: {m.intervalStep + 1}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-[#0F172A] leading-snug">
                        {q.questionText}
                      </h3>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => toggleExpand(m.id)}
                        className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 text-blue-600 text-xs font-bold transition-colors flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <span>{isExpanded ? 'সংক্ষেপ করুন' : 'ব্যাখ্যা দেখুন'}</span>
                        {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Student Mistake Self-Diagnosis Reason Selection */}
                  <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500">ভুলের কারণ চিহ্নিত করো:</span>
                    {reasonsList.map((r) => (
                      <button
