              {/* Error Notice */}
              {errorMsg && (
                <div className="p-3 mb-4 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
                  <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                  <span>{errorMsg}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Main Form */}
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3.5">
                
                {isSignUp && (
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      {lang === 'bn' ? 'সম্পূর্ণ নাম' : 'Full Name'}
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <User className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-3" />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={name}
                        onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder={isStudent ? 'তানভীর আহমেদ' : 'মোঃ রফিকুল ইসলাম'}
                        className="w-full pl-10 pr-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-all"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    {lang === 'bn' ? 'ইমেইল বা ইউজারনেম' : 'Email or Username'}
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-3" />
                    <input
                      type="email"
                      required
                      value={email}
                      onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder={isStudent ? 'student@example.com' : 'teacher@school.edu.bd'}
                      className="w-full pl-10 pr-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-all"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                    <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
                      {lang === 'bn' ? 'পাসওয়ার্ড' : 'Password'}
                    </label>
                    {!isSignUp && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => alert(lang === 'bn' ? 'পাসওয়ার্ড রিসেট লিংক আপনার ইমেইলে পাঠানো হবে।' : 'Password reset link will be sent to your email.')}
                        className="text-[11px] text-blue-600 hover:underline"
                      >
                        {lang === 'bn' ? 'পাসওয়ার্ড ভুলে গেছেন?' : 'Forgot password?'}
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-3" />
                    <input
                      type="password"
                      required
                      value={password}
                      onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="••••••••"
                      className="w-full pl-10 pr-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-all"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                {isSignUp && !isStudent && (
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      {lang === 'bn' ? 'শিক্ষা প্রতিষ্ঠানের নাম' : 'Institution / School Name'}
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Building className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-3" />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={institution}
                        onChange={(e) => setInstitution(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ঢাকা রেসিডেনসিয়াল মডেল কলেজ"
                        className="w-full pl-10 pr-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-all"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {!isSignUp && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      id="rememberMe"
                      checked={rememberMe}
                      onChange={(e) => setRememberMe(e.target.checked)}
                      className="w-4 h-4 rounded-sm text-blue-600 focus:ring-blue-500 border-slate-300"
                    />
                    <label htmlFor="rememberMe" className="text-xs text-slate-600 cursor-pointer">
                      {lang === 'bn' ? 'আমাকে মনে রাখুন' : 'Remember me'}
                    </label>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Submit Button */}
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className={`w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm text-white shadow-md transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer ${
                    isStudent
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-indigo-700 shadow-blue-500/25'
                      : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-purple-700 hover:to-indigo-700 shadow-purple-500/25'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>
                    {loading
                      ? (lang === 'bn' ? 'যাচাই করা হচ্ছে...' : 'Verifying...')
                      : isSignUp
                      ? (lang === 'bn' ? 'একাউন্ট তৈরি করুন' : 'Create Account')
                      : (lang === 'bn' ? 'লগইন করুন' : 'Login')}
                  </span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </form>

              {/* Or Continue with Google */}
              <div className="my-4 flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-slate-200" />
                <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-semibold">Or continue with</span>
                <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-slate-200" />
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleGoogleLogin}
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <svg className="w-4 h-4" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path
                    fill="#4285F4"
                    d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z"
                  />
                  <path
                    fill="#34A853"
                    d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z"
                  />
                  <path
                    fill="#FBBC05"
                    d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.06H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.94l2.85-2.22.81-.63z"
                  />
                  <path
                    fill="#EA4335"
                    d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.06l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.52 6.16-4.52z"
                  />
                </svg>
                <span>{lang === 'bn' ? 'Google দিয়ে প্রবেশ করুন' : 'Continue with Google'}</span>
              </button>

              {isStudent && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleGuestStudentEntry}
                  className="w-full mt-2.5 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-blue-50/80 hover:bg-blue-100/80 border border-blue-200/80 text-blue-700 font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
                  <span>{lang === 'bn' ? 'গেস্ট হিসেবে সরাসরি ড্যাশবোর্ডে প্রবেশ করুন' : 'Continue as Guest Student to Dashboard'}</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Bottom Footer Switcher */}
            <div className="mt-5 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
              {isSignUp ? (
                <>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'ইতিমধ্যে একাউন্ট আছে? ' : 'Already have an account? '}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => { setIsSignUp(false); setErrorMsg(null); }}
                    className="text-blue-600 font-bold hover:underline"
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? 'লগইন করুন' : 'Sign In'}
                  </button>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'কোনো একাউন্ট নেই? ' : "Don't have an account? "}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => { setIsSignUp(true); setErrorMsg(null); }}
                    className="text-blue-600 font-bold hover:underline"
                  >
                    {isStudent
                      ? (lang === 'bn' ? 'নিবন্ধন করুন' : 'Sign Up')
                      : (lang === 'bn' ? 'শিক্ষক হিসেবে নিবন্ধন' : 'Sign Up as Teacher')}
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

===== END FILE: src/components/AuthModal.tsx =====

===== BEGIN FILE 7/32: src/components/HistoricalAnalysisView.tsx =====
import { apiFetch as fetch } from '../utils/api.ts';
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  ShieldAlert,
  Upload,
  CheckCircle2,
  FileText,
  AlertCircle,
  Plus,
  ArrowRight
} from 'lucide-react';

interface HistoricalPaper {
  id: string;
  year: number;
  board: string;
  subject: string;
  chapter: string;
  topic: string;
  verifiedCount: number;
  status: 'verified' | 'pending';
}

interface HistoricalAnalysisResponse {
  hasVerifiedData: boolean;
  disclaimer: string;
  statusMessage: string;
  papers: HistoricalPaper[];
}

interface HistoricalAnalysisViewProps {
  userRole: string;
  onBack: () => void;
}

export const HistoricalAnalysisView: React.FC<HistoricalAnalysisViewProps> = ({
  userRole,
  onBack,
}) => {
  const [data, setData] = useState<HistoricalAnalysisResponse | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  // Admin Import Form State
  const [showImportForm, setShowImportForm] = useState(false);
  const [importYear, setImportYear] = useState('2024');
  const [importBoard, setImportBoard] = useState('ঢাকা');
  const [importSubject, setImportSubject] = useState('সাধারণ গণিত');
  const [importChapter, setImportChapter] = useState('বীজগাণিতিক রাশি');
  const [sampleQuestionsText, setSampleQuestionsText] = useState('[]');
  const [importError, setImportError] = useState('');
  const [isImporting, setIsImporting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/historical-analysis')
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((resData) => setData(resData))
      .catch((err) => console.error(err))
      .finally(() => setIsLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const handleAdminImport = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsImporting(true);
    try {
      setImportError('');
      const questions = JSON.parse(sampleQuestionsText);
      if (!Array.isArray(questions) || !questions.length) throw new Error('পূর্ণ প্রশ্নের JSON array দিন।');

      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/import-questions', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          year: importYear,
          board: importBoard,
          subject: importSubject,
          chapter: importChapter,
          questions,
        }),
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        alert('প্রশ্নগুলো সংরক্ষিত হয়েছে। Admin dashboard থেকে যাচাই ও অনুমোদন করুন।');
        setShowImportForm(false);
        // Refresh
        const refreshRes = await fetch('/api/historical-analysis');
        const refreshedData = await refreshRes.json();
        setData(refreshedData);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setImportError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setIsImporting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-8 space-y-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-slate-200">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-md mb-2">
            <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" />
            <span>বোর্ড প্রশ্ন প্যাটার্ন বিশ্লেষণ (Board Pattern Analysis)</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            যাচাইকৃত ঐতিহাসিক বোর্ড প্রশ্ন বিশ্লেষণ
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            ExamDNA BD শিক্ষামূলক সততায় বিশ্বাসী। আমরা ভুয়া বা কাল্পনিক প্রশ্নের ফ্রিকোয়েন্সি প্রদর্শন করি না।
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={onBack}
            className="px-4 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-xl"
          >
            ফিরে যান
          </button>
          {userRole === 'admin' && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowImportForm(true)}
              className="px-4 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
            >
              <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>বোর্ড প্রশ্নপত্র ইমপোর্ট</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Honest Academic Standard Banner */}
      <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-900 font-bold text-sm">
          <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
          <span>একাডেমিক সততা ও তথ্যের স্বচ্ছতা সংক্রান্ত নীতিমালা</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
          {data?.disclaimer ||
            'ExamDNA BD শিক্ষামূলক সততা বজায় রাখে। পর্যাপ্ত যাচাইকৃত প্রশ্ন সংরক্ষিত না থাকলে কোনো কৃত্রিম বা ভুয়া ফ্রিকোয়েন্সি প্রদর্শন করা হয় না।'}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Main Status Container */}
      {!data?.hasVerifiedData ? (
        /* Honest Empty State */
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-12 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-4">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-7 h-7" />
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            {data?.statusMessage || 'পর্যাপ্ত যাচাইকৃত বোর্ড প্রশ্নের তথ্য নেই।'}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 leading-relaxed">
            কোনো বোর্ড পরীক্ষা না নিয়ে বা প্রশ্নপত্র আইনসম্মতভাবে সংগ্রহ ও শিক্ষক মণ্ডলী কর্তৃক যাচাই না করে ভুয়া ঐতিহাসিক অ্যানালিটিক্স প্রদর্শন করা শিক্ষার্থীর জন্য ক্ষতিকর। অ্যাডমিন কর্তৃক প্রশ্নপত্র নথিভুক্ত হলে তা এখানে প্রকাশিত হবে।
          </p>

          <div className="pt-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowImportForm(true)}
              className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
            >
              (অ্যাডমিন মোডে) প্রথম বোর্ড প্রশ্নপত্র নথিভুক্ত করুন
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* Verified Imported Papers List */
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-base">
              যাচাইকৃত নথিভুক্ত প্রশ্নপত্র ({data.papers.length} টি)
            </h3>
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded">
              যাচাই সম্পন্ন
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {data.papers.map((p) => (
              <div
                key={p.id}
                className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs"
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">
                    {p.board} বোর্ড {p.year} — {p.subject}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-slate-500 mt-0.5">অধ্যায়: {p.chapter}</div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <div className="font-bold text-emerald-600 font-mono-numbers">
                    {p.verifiedCount}টি প্রশ্ন অনুমোদিত
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">স্ট্যাটাস: লাইভ প্রশ্নব্যাংক</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Admin Import Modal */}
      {showImportForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <form
            onSubmit={handleAdminImport}
            className="bg-white rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 space-y-5"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-base">
                বোর্ড পরীক্ষার প্রশ্নপত্র ইনডেক্স ও যাচাই
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowImportForm(false)}
                className="text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 font-medium"
              >
                বাতিল
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">বোর্ড</label>
                <select
                  value={importBoard}
                  onChange={(e) => setImportBoard(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50"
                >
                  <option value="ঢাকা">ঢাকা বোর্ড</option>
                  <option value="চট্টগ্রাম">চট্টগ্রাম বোর্ড</option>
                  <option value="রাজশাহী">রাজশাহী বোর্ড</option>
                  <option value="যশোর">যশোর বোর্ড</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">বছর</label>
                <select
                  value={importYear}
                  onChange={(e) => setImportYear(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50"
                >
                  <option value="2024">২০২৪</option>
                  <option value="2023">২০২৩</option>
                  <option value="2022">২০২২</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">বিষয় ও অধ্যায়</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                readOnly
                value={`${importSubject} - ${importChapter}`}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                প্রশ্নের JSON array (options, correctAnswerIndex 0–3, explanation, topic, difficulty সহ)
              </label>
              {importError && <p role="alert" className="text-red-700">{importError}</p>}
              <textarea
                rows={3}
                value={sampleQuestionsText}
                onChange={(e) => setSampleQuestionsText(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600 font-mono"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50 rounded-xl text-[11px] text-blue-900">
              *সংরক্ষিত প্রশ্নগুলো যাচাইয়ের অপেক্ষায় থাকবে। Admin approval-এর পরে পরীক্ষায় ব্যবহার হবে।
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowImportForm(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl"
              >
                বাতিল
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isImporting}
                className="px-5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 rounded-xl shadow-md disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {isImporting ? 'সংরক্ষণ হচ্ছে...' : 'যাচাই ও ইমপোর্ট সম্পন্ন করুন'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

===== END FILE: src/components/HistoricalAnalysisView.tsx =====

===== BEGIN FILE 8/32: src/components/StudentDashboard.tsx =====
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { StudentProfile, UserMistakeEntry } from '../types';
import { getActiveDisplayStreak } from '../utils/streakCalculator';
import {
  Sparkles,
  Flame,
  Target,
  BrainCircuit,
  BookOpen,
  Award,
  Users,
  ArrowRight,
  TrendingUp,
  AlertCircle,
  HelpCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Search,
  Bell,
  Calculator,
  Atom,
  Swords,
  Trophy,
  BarChart2,
  Check,
  Play,
  Star,
  ChevronRight,
  LayoutDashboard,
  FileText,
  User,
  ShieldCheck,
  Plus
} from 'lucide-react';

interface StudentDashboardProps {
  profile: StudentProfile;
  currentUser?: any;
  mistakes: UserMistakeEntry[];
  examHistory: any[];
  onReviewResult: (result: any) => void;
  totalExamsCount: number;
  averageAccuracy: number;
  onStartDiagnostic: () => void;
  onStartAdaptive: () => void;
  onOpenMistakes: () => void;
  onOpenChallenge: () => void;
  onOpenAiCoach: () => void;
  onOpenDailyActivities?: () => void;
  onJoinBatch: (code: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  onNavigateToSetup?: () => void;
}

export const StudentDashboard: React.FC<StudentDashboardProps> = ({
  profile,
  currentUser,
  mistakes = [],
  examHistory = [],
  onReviewResult,
  totalExamsCount = 0,
  averageAccuracy = 0,
  onStartDiagnostic,
  onStartAdaptive,
  onOpenMistakes,
  onOpenChallenge,
  onOpenAiCoach,
  onOpenDailyActivities,
  onJoinBatch,
  onNavigateToSetup,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'dashboard' | 'exams' | 'mistakes' | 'adaptive' | 'challenge' | 'progress' | 'achievements' | 'profile'>('dashboard');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [batchCodeInput, setBatchCodeInput] = useState('');
  const [joinMsg, setJoinMsg] = useState<{ text: string; type: 'success' | 'error' } | null>(null);

  const safeMistakes = Array.isArray(mistakes) ? mistakes : [];
  const dueTodayCount = safeMistakes.filter(m => m?.nextReviewAt && new Date(m.nextReviewAt).getTime() <= Date.now()).length;

  const userKey = currentUser?.id || profile?.id || 'guest';
  const avatarPhoto = currentUser?.photoUrl || profile?.photoUrl || (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem(`examdna_student_photo_${userKey}`) : null);
  
  const storedStreak = currentUser?.streakDays ?? profile?.streakDays ?? 0;
  const lastActivity = currentUser?.lastActivityDate ?? profile?.lastActivityDate;
  const activeStreak = getActiveDisplayStreak(storedStreak, lastActivity);

  const handleJoin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!batchCodeInput.trim()) return;
    try {
      const ok = await onJoinBatch(batchCodeInput.trim());
      if (ok) {
        setJoinMsg({ text: 'সফলভাবে ব্যাচে যুক্ত হয়েছো!', type: 'success' });
        setBatchCodeInput('');
      } else {
        setJoinMsg({ text: 'ব্যাচ কোডটি পাওয়া যায়নি।', type: 'error' });
      }
    } catch {
      setJoinMsg({ text: 'সংযোগ বিচ্ছিন্ন। পুনরায় চেষ্টা করুন।', type: 'error' });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] flex flex-col lg:flex-row">
      
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      {/* 1. LEFT VERTICAL SIDEBAR (Matching Screen 5)                   */}
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      <aside className="w-full lg:w-64 bg-white border-r border-slate-200/80 p-5 flex flex-col justify-between shrink-0">
        <div>
          {/* Logo */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-2 py-3 mb-6">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-black text-sm shadow-md shadow-blue-500/25">
              DNA
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-base font-black text-[#0F172A] flex items-center gap-1">
                <span>ExamDNA</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-blue-600 bg-blue-50 px-1 py-0.5 rounded border border-blue-200">
                  BD
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-400">Student Portal</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Navigation Items */}
          <nav className="space-y-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('dashboard')}
              className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold transition-all text-left ${
                activeTab === 'dashboard'
                  ? 'bg-blue-50/80 text-blue-600 border border-blue-200/80 shadow-2xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              <LayoutDashboard className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Dashboard</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                if (onNavigateToSetup) onNavigateToSetup();
                else onStartDiagnostic();
              }}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-all text-left"
            >
              <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Take Exam</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('exams')}
              className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold transition-all text-left ${
                activeTab === 'exams'
                  ? 'bg-blue-50/80 text-blue-600 border border-blue-200/80 shadow-2xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50'

                    <Star className="w-4 h-4 fill-emerald-500" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">Top Performer</div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/80">
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 mx-auto rounded-full bg-purple-100 text-purple-600 flex items-center justify-center mb-1.5">
                    <Trophy className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">Challenge Master</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

          </div>

          {/* Right Column (5 Cols): Circular Progress Gauge & Join Batch */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-6">
            
            {/* প্রস্তুতির উদাহরণ (ব্যক্তিগত ফলাফল নয়) Circular Gauge Card matching Screen 5 */}
            <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl border border-blue-100/90 shadow-xl shadow-blue-500/5 text-center">
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-[#0F172A] mb-4 text-left">Your Progress</h3>

              {/* Circular Ring Gauge */}
              <div className="relative w-44 h-44 mx-auto flex items-center justify-center my-3">
                <svg className="w-full h-full transform -rotate-90" viewBox="0 0 100 100">
                  {/* Background Track */}
                  <circle
                    cx="50"
                    cy="50"
                    r="40"
                    stroke="#EFF6FF"
                    strokeWidth="10"
                    fill="transparent"
                  />
                  {/* Progress Indicator */}
                  <circle
                    cx="50"
                    cy="50"
                    r="40"
                    stroke="#2563EB"
                    strokeWidth="10"
                    strokeDasharray="251.2"
                    strokeDashoffset={251.2 * (1 - (totalExamsCount > 0 ? Math.min(1, averageAccuracy / 100) : 0))}
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    fill="transparent"
                  />
                </svg>
                <div className="absolute flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
                  <span className="text-3xl font-black text-[#0F172A] font-mono-numbers">
                    {totalExamsCount > 0 ? `${averageAccuracy}%` : '০%'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    {totalExamsCount > 0 ? 'সার্বিক নির্ভুলতা' : 'কোনো পরীক্ষা নেই'}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Breakdown Bars */}
              {totalExamsCount > 0 ? (
                <div className="space-y-3 mt-6 text-left">
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
                      <span>সাধারণ গণিত (General Math)</span>
                      <span className="text-blue-600 font-mono-numbers">{averageAccuracy}%</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div className="bg-blue-600 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ width: `${averageAccuracy}%` }} />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
                      <span>পদার্থবিজ্ঞান ও বিজ্ঞান (Science)</span>
                      <span className="text-cyan-600 font-mono-numbers">{Math.max(0, Math.min(100, averageAccuracy - 4))}%</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div className="bg-cyan-500 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ width: `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, averageAccuracy - 4))}%` }} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-6 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 text-left">
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-medium">
                    তোমার কোনো পরীক্ষার রেকর্ড এখনো সংরক্ষিত নেই। বিষয়ভিত্তিক নির্ভুলতা ও অগ্রগতি দেখতে প্রথম পরীক্ষাটি সম্পন্ন করো।
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onStartAdaptive}
                className="w-full mt-6 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 font-bold text-xs transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
              >
                <span>Improve Weak Topics</span>
                <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Join Teacher Batch Card */}
            <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl border border-blue-100 shadow-2xs">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-black text-[#0F172A] mb-1">Join Teacher's Batch</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
                Enter your teacher's batch code to participate in scheduled class exams.
              </p>

              <form onSubmit={handleJoin} className="space-y-3">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={batchCodeInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setBatchCodeInput(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
                  placeholder="e.g. SSC2027M"
                  maxLength={10}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs font-mono font-bold tracking-wider uppercase text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500"
                />

                {joinMsg && (
                  <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold ${
                    joinMsg.type === 'success' ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-red-50 text-red-700'
                  }`}>
                    {joinMsg.text}
                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Join Batch
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>

          </div>

        </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* TAB 2: MY EXAMS / COMPLETED RESULTS */}
        {activeTab === 'exams' && (
          <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-200">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-[#0F172A]">আমার পরীক্ষাসমূহ ও ফলাফল (My Exams & Results)</h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">পূর্বে সম্পন্ন করা পরীক্ষার বিস্তারিত স্কোর, নির্ভুলতা ও পর্যালোচনা</p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (onNavigateToSetup) onNavigateToSetup();
                  else onStartDiagnostic();
                }}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-xs transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>নতুন পরীক্ষা শুরু করুন</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* List of Previous Exams */}
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {examHistory.length === 0 && <p className="p-5 text-slate-500">এখনও সংরক্ষিত পরীক্ষা নেই। Demo ফলাফল ব্যক্তিগত history-তে যোগ হয় না।</p>}
              {examHistory.map(r => ({ ...r, id: r.id || r.examId, title: r.title || 'MCQ পরীক্ষা', subject: r.questionsReview?.[0]?.subject || '', accuracy: r.accuracyPercentage, total: r.totalQuestions, date: new Date(r.submittedAt).toLocaleString('bn-BD', {timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'}), timeSpent: Math.round(r.timeSpentSeconds / 60) + ' মিনিট', original: r })).map((ex) => (
                <div
                  key={ex.id}
                  className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-blue-100 shadow-2xs hover:shadow-md transition-all flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center border border-blue-100 font-black text-sm shrink-0">
                      {ex.accuracy}%
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-blue-200">
                          {ex.subject}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-medium">· {ex.date}</span>
                      </div>
                      <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] mt-1">{ex.title}</h4>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                        স্কোর: <strong className="text-slate-800">{ex.score} / {ex.total}</strong> · সময়: {ex.timeSpent}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onReviewResult(ex.original)}
                      className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold border border-slate-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      ভুলসমূহ রিভিউ করো
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (onNavigateToSetup) onNavigateToSetup();
                        else onStartDiagnostic();
                      }}
                      className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      পুনরায় দাও
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'progress' && <section className="p-6 bg-white rounded-xl"><h2>বিষয়ভিত্তিক ফলাফল</h2>{[...new Set(examHistory.flatMap(r => (r.questionsReview || []).map((q: any) => q.subject)))].map(subject => {
          const questions = examHistory.flatMap(r => r.questionsReview || []).filter(q => q.subject === subject);
          const correct = questions.filter(q => q.status === 'correct').length;
          return <p key={String(subject)} className="py-3">{String(subject)}: {correct}/{questions.length} ({Math.round(correct/questions.length*100)}%)</p>;
        })}{!examHistory.length && <p>পরীক্ষা সম্পন্ন হলে অগ্রগতি দেখাবে।</p>}</section>}
        {activeTab === 'achievements' && <section className="p-6 bg-white rounded-xl"><h2>অর্জন</h2><p>{examHistory.length ? 'প্রথম পরীক্ষা সম্পন্ন ✓' : 'প্রথম পরীক্ষা এখনও সম্পন্ন হয়নি'}</p><p>{activeStreak >= 5 ? '৫ দিনের ধারাবাহিকতা ✓' : '৫ দিনের ধারাবাহিকতা এখনও অর্জিত হয়নি'}</p></section>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

===== END FILE: src/components/StudentDashboard.tsx =====

===== BEGIN FILE 9/32: src/components/AdminDashboard.tsx =====
import { auth, signInWithEmailAndPassword, getFirestoreUserProfile } from '../config/firebase.ts';
import { apiFetch as fetch } from '../utils/api.ts';
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  ShieldAlert,
  ShieldCheck,
  Users,
  GraduationCap,
  School,
  FileQuestion,
  CheckCircle2,
  XCircle,
  Database,
  Cpu,
  RefreshCw,
  LogOut,
  AlertTriangle,
  Upload,
  BookOpen,
  Lock,
  ArrowRight,
  FileText,
  Layers,
  Sparkles,
  Clock,
  MinusCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { translations, Language } from '../utils/i18n';
import type { UserAccount } from '../types';

interface AdminDashboardProps {
  lang: Language;
  currentUser: UserAccount | null;
  onLogout: () => void;
  onAdminAuthSuccess: (user: UserAccount, token: string) => void;
  onExitAdminView: () => void;
}

export const AdminDashboard: React.FC<AdminDashboardProps> = ({
  lang,
  currentUser,
  onLogout,
  onAdminAuthSuccess,
  onExitAdminView,
}) => {
  const t = translations[lang];
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'overview' | 'teachers' | 'questions' | 'board_papers' | 'system'>('overview');

  // Admin login form states (when not authenticated as admin)
  const [adminEmail, setAdminEmail] = useState('');
  const [adminPassword, setAdminPassword] = useState('');
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

  // Admin data states
  const [overview, setOverview] = useState<any>(null);
  const [usersList, setUsersList] = useState<UserAccount[]>([]);
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [systemStats, setSystemStats] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loadingData, setLoadingData] = useState(false);

  // Historical board paper input & Board Matrix Tracker
  const [paperYear, setPaperYear] = useState('2024');
  const [paperBoard, setPaperBoard] = useState('ঢাকা');
  const [paperSubject, setPaperSubject] = useState('সাধারণ গণিত');
  const [paperChapter, setPaperChapter] = useState('বীজগাণিতিক রাশি');
  const [paperImportStatus, setPaperImportStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Board Coverage Matrix & Ingestion Pipeline States
  const [boardCoverage, setBoardCoverage] = useState<any>(null);
  const [matrixSubject, setMatrixSubject] = useState<'সাধারণ গণিত' | 'পদার্থবিজ্ঞান' | 'রসায়ন' | 'জীববিজ্ঞান'>('সাধারণ গণিত');
  const [ingestRawText, setIngestRawText] = useState('');
  const [ingestBoard, setIngestBoard] = useState('ঢাকা');
  const [ingestYear, setIngestYear] = useState('2024');
  const [ingestSubject, setIngestSubject] = useState('সাধারণ গণিত');
  const [ingestChapter, setIngestChapter] = useState('বীজগাণিতিক রাশি');
  const [ingestTopic, setIngestTopic] = useState('বোর্ড নৈর্ব্যক্তিক');
  const [ingestFormat, setIngestFormat] = useState<'text' | 'json'>('json');
  const [isIngesting, setIsIngesting] = useState(false);
  const [ingestFeedback, setIngestFeedback] = useState<any | null>(null);

  const isAdmin = currentUser?.role === 'admin';

  // Fetch admin data on load or tab change if authenticated
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAdmin) {
      fetchAdminData();
    }
  }, [isAdmin]);

  const getAdminHeaders = () => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('examdna_token') || currentUser?.token || '';
    return {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    };
  };

  const fetchAdminData = async () => {
    setLoadingData(true);
    try {
      const headers = getAdminHeaders();
      const [overviewRes, usersRes, questionsRes, statsRes, coverageRes] = await Promise.all([
        fetch('/api/admin/overview', { headers }),
        fetch('/api/admin/users', { headers }),
        fetch('/api/admin/questions', { headers }),
        fetch('/api/admin/system-stats', { headers }),
        fetch('/api/admin/board-coverage', { headers }),
      ]);

      if (overviewRes.ok) setOverview(await overviewRes.json());
      if (usersRes.ok) setUsersList(await usersRes.json());
      if (questionsRes.ok) setQuestions(await questionsRes.json());
      if (statsRes.ok) setSystemStats(await statsRes.json());
      if (coverageRes.ok) setBoardCoverage(await coverageRes.json());
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error fetching admin data:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingData(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePipelineIngest = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!ingestRawText.trim()) return;
    setIsIngesting(true);
    setIngestFeedback(null);

    try {
      let payload: any = {
        board: ingestBoard,
        year: parseInt(ingestYear, 10),
        subject: ingestSubject,
        defaultChapter: ingestChapter,
        defaultTopic: ingestTopic,
        autoApprove: false,
      };

      if (ingestFormat === 'json') {
        try {
          payload.jsonQuestions = JSON.parse(ingestRawText);
        } catch {
          setIngestFeedback({ error: 'ভুল JSON ফরম্যাট। সঠিক JSON অ্যারে প্রদান করুন।' });
          setIsIngesting(false);
          return;
        }
      } else {
        payload.rawText = ingestRawText;
      }

      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/pipeline/ingest', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAdminHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setIngestFeedback({ error: data.error || 'আমদানি ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।' });
      } else {
        setIngestFeedback({
          success: true,
          parsedTotal: data.parsedTotal,
          importedCount: data.importedCount,
          duplicateCount: data.duplicateCount,
          totalBankSize: data.totalBankSize,
        });
        setIngestRawText('');
        fetchAdminData();
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setIngestFeedback({ error: err.message || 'নেটওয়ার্ক ত্রুটি' });
    } finally {
      setIsIngesting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateSlotStatus = async (board: string, year: number, subject: string, status: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/board-coverage/status', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAdminHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ board, year, subject, status }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        fetchAdminData();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to update slot status:', err);
    }
  };

  // Secure Admin Login Handler
  const handleAdminLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setAuthError(null);
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    try {
      const credential = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, adminEmail.trim(), adminPassword);
      const user = await getFirestoreUserProfile(credential.user.uid);
      if (!user || user.role !== 'admin') throw new Error('Admin access required.');
      onAdminAuthSuccess(user, await credential.user.getIdToken());
    } catch (err: any) {
      setAuthError(err.message || 'Access denied');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  // Teacher verification toggle
  const handleTeacherVerification = async (teacherId: string, status: 'approved' | 'rejected') => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/verify-teacher', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAdminHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ teacherId, status }),
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        // Refresh users list
        fetchAdminData();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // Question moderation (Approve / Reject)
  const handleModerateQuestion = async (questionId: string, action: 'approve' | 'reject') => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/questions/moderate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAdminHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ questionId, action }),
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        setQuestions((prev) =>
          action === 'reject'
            ? prev.filter((q) => q.id !== questionId)
            : prev.map((q) => (q.id === questionId ? { ...q, verificationStatus: 'verified' } : q))
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // Import board questions batch
  const handleImportBoardPaper = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setPaperImportStatus(lang === 'bn' ? 'আমদানি প্রক্রিয়াধীন...' : 'Importing...');
    try {
      setPaperImportStatus('Use the JSON import panel with actual source questions and explicit answer keys.');
    } catch (err) {
      setPaperImportStatus(lang === 'bn' ? 'আমদানি ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।' : 'Import failed.');
    }
  };

  // If NOT authenticated as admin, render the protected login gate
  if (!isAdmin) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div className="bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 max-w-md w-full rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-2xl relative text-slate-100">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-500/30 text-blue-400 text-xs font-semibold">
              <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>ExamDNA Security Gateway</span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onExitAdminView}
              className="text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'হোমে ফিরে যান' : 'Back to Home'}
            </button>
          </div>

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2">
            {lang === 'bn' ? 'সংরক্ষিত অ্যাডমিন পোর্টাল' : 'Restricted Admin Portal'}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-6 leading-relaxed">
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'এই রুটটি শুধুমাত্র প্ল্যাটফর্ম প্রতিষ্ঠাতা ও সিস্টেম প্রশাসকের জন্য সংরক্ষিত। ব্যাকএন্ড যাচাইকরণের মাধ্যমে কঠোরভাবে প্রবেশাধিকার নিয়ন্ত্রিত।'
              : 'This route is strictly restricted to platform founders and verified administrators. Access is enforced by server-side claims.'}
          </p>

          {authError && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-red-950/50 border border-red-500/40 text-red-300 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-red-400" />
              <span>{authError}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleAdminLogin} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-300 mb-1">
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'প্রশাসক ইমেইল (Founder Email)' : 'Administrator Email'}
              </label>
              <input
                type="email"
                required
                value={adminEmail}
                onChange={(e) => setAdminEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="founder@domain.com"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-300 mb-1">
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'সিক্রেট কি বা পাসওয়ার্ড' : 'Secret Key / Password'}
              </label>
              <input
                type="password"
                required
                value={adminPassword}
                onChange={(e) => setAdminPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="••••••••••••"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              className="w-full py-3 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/20 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <span>{isSubmitting ? (lang === 'bn' ? 'যাচাই করা হচ্ছে...' : 'Authenticating...') : (lang === 'bn' ? 'প্রবেশ করুন' : 'Verify & Enter')}</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-700 text-center">
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
              ExamDNA BD &bull; Powered by Gemini 3.8 Flash Engine
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Render Authenticated Admin Dashboard
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900">
      {/* Top Admin Bar */}
      <header className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200 sticky top-0 z-30 shadow-xs">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm shadow-sm shadow-blue-500/30">
              DNA
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base">ExamDNA BD</span>
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">
                  Admin Master Console
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'সিস্টেম প্রশাসন ও এআই কনফিগারেশন' : 'System Administration & AI Governance'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={fetchAdminData}
              className="p-2 text-slate-500 hover:text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
              title="Refresh Telemetry"
            >
              <RefreshCw className={`w-4 h-4 ${loadingData ? 'animate-spin text-blue-600' : ''}`} />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onExitAdminView}
              className="text-xs text-slate-600 hover:text-blue-600 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 hover:border-blue-200 transition-colors"
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'ওয়েবসাইটে ফিরে যান' : 'Exit to App'}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onLogout}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{t.navLogout}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Admin Navigation Tabs */}
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 flex space-x-1 sm:space-x-4 border-t border-slate-100 overflow-x-auto py-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('overview')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all shrink-0 ${
              activeTab === 'overview' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
            }`}
          >
            {lang === 'bn' ? 'ওভারভিউ ও অ্যানালিটিক্স' : 'Overview & Analytics'}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('teachers')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              activeTab === 'teachers' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{lang === 'bn' ? 'শিক্ষক যাচাইকরণ' : 'Teacher Verification'}</span>
            {overview?.pendingTeachers > 0 && (
              <span className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-amber-500 text-white text-[10px] flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                {overview.pendingTeachers}
              </span>
            )}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('questions')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all shrink-0 ${
              activeTab === 'questions' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
            }`}
          >
            {lang === 'bn' ? 'প্রশ্ন ব্যাংক মডারেশন' : 'Question Bank Moderation'}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('board_papers')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all shrink-0 ${
              activeTab === 'board_papers' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
            }`}
          >
            {lang === 'bn' ? 'বোর্ড পেপার আমদানি' : 'Board Paper Manager'}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('system')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all shrink-0 ${
              activeTab === 'system' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
            }`}
          >
            {lang === 'bn' ? 'সিস্টেম ও এআই কোটা' : 'System & AI Telemetry'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Admin Content Container */}
      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8">
        
        {/* TAB 1: OVERVIEW */}
        {activeTab === 'overview' && (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {/* Stat Cards */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
              <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">{lang === 'bn' ? 'মোট নিবন্ধিত শিক্ষার্থী' : 'Total Students'}</span>

                    <GraduationCap className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">

                </div>
              </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">{lang === 'bn' ? 'মোট নিবন্ধিত শিক্ষক' : 'Total Teachers'}</span>
                  <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600">
                    <School className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{overview?.totalTeachers ?? 1}</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-amber-600 font-medium mt-1">
                  {overview?.pendingTeachers || 0} {lang === 'bn' ? 'অনুমোদনের অপেক্ষায়' : 'pending approval'}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">{lang === 'bn' ? 'যাচাইকৃত প্রশ্ন ব্যাংক' : 'Question Bank Size'}</span>
                  <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600">
                    <Database className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{overview?.totalQuestions ?? questions.length}</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-medium mt-1">
                  {overview?.verifiedQuestions || 0} {lang === 'bn' ? 'এনসিটিবি যাচাইকৃত' : 'verified'}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">{lang === 'bn' ? 'এআই মডেল স্ট্যাটাস' : 'Active AI Model'}</span>
                  <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-purple-50 text-purple-600">
                    <Cpu className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">gemini-3.8-flash</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
                  <span>সরাসরি ইন্টিগ্রেশন চালু</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Quick Actions & Recent Users */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">

                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span>{lang === 'bn' ? 'সাম্প্রতিক ব্যবহারকারীগণ' : 'Recent Registered Users'}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-blue-600 font-normal">মোট {usersList.length} জন</span>
                </h3>
                <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {usersList.slice(0, 5).map((u) => (
                    <div key={u.id} className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">{u.name}</span>
                          <span className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold uppercase ${
                            u.role === 'admin' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700' : u.role === 'teacher' ? 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700' : 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700'
                          }`}>
                            {u.role}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{u.email}</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-right">
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{u.institution || u.track || 'SSC 2027'}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mb-4">
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'সিস্টেম নিরাপত্তা ও অডিট লগ' : 'System Security & Audit Summary'}
                </h3>
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 flex items-start gap-3">
                    <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">
                        {lang === 'bn' ? 'কাস্টম রোল বেসড অ্যাক্সেস কন্ট্রোল (RBAC) সক্রিয়' : 'Custom RBAC Enforced'}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 leading-relaxed">
                        {lang === 'bn'
                          ? 'শুধুমাত্র ব্যাকএন্ডে যাচাইকৃত প্রতিষ্ঠাতা ইমেইল ও টোকেন ধারী অ্যাডমিন রুটের ডেটা দেখতে সক্ষম।'
                          : 'Only a server-authorized administrator can inspect admin datasets.'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 flex items-start gap-3">
                    <Database className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">
                        {lang === 'bn' ? 'এনসিটিবি পাঠ্যক্রম সুরক্ষা ও বোর্ড প্যাটার্ন গার্ড' : 'NCTB Curriculum Safety Guard'}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 leading-relaxed">
                        {lang === 'bn'
                          ? 'স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে ভুল উত্তর সূচক ও বিভ্রান্তিকর অপশন পরিহার করে নির্ভুল পরীক্ষা নিশ্চয়তা দেওয়া হচ্ছে।'
                          : 'Deterministic solution verification applied to all published and diagnostic tests.'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* TAB 2: TEACHERS */}
        {activeTab === 'teachers' && (

              {lang === 'bn' ? 'শিক্ষক তালিকা ও ভেরিফিকেশন ম্যানেজমেন্ট' : 'Teacher Verification Management'}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-6">
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'শিক্ষকদের প্রশ্নপত্র প্রকাশ ও স্টুডেন্ট ব্যাচ তৈরির পূর্বে অ্যাডমিন ভেরিফিকেশন অনুমোদন করুন।'
                : 'Review credentials and approve teacher accounts before granting official exam publishing rights.'}

                    <th className="py-3 px-4">{lang === 'bn' ? 'ইমেইল' : 'Email'}</th>

                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-500">{teacher.email}</td>
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{teacher.institution || '—'}</td>
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{teacher.subject || 'সাধারণ গণিত'}</td>
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                          {teacher.isVerifiedTeacher ? (
                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 text-[11px] font-semibold">
                              <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />
                              <span>{lang === 'bn' ? 'যাচাইকৃত' : 'Verified'}</span>
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-[11px] font-semibold">
                              <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-600" />
                              <span>{lang === 'bn' ? 'অপেক্ষমাণ' : 'Pending'}</span>
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right space-x-2">
                          {!teacher.isVerifiedTeacher ? (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleTeacherVerification(teacher.id, 'approved')}
                              className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs"
                            >
                              {lang === 'bn' ? 'অনুমোদন করুন' : 'Approve'}

                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleTeacherVerification(teacher.id, 'rejected')}

                              {lang === 'bn' ? 'অনুমোদন স্থগিত' : 'Revoke'}

                      </tr>
                    ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* TAB 3: QUESTIONS */}
        {activeTab === 'questions' && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-6 shadow-xs">
